## Account

### catalogServices.create

`account.catalogServices.create`

Create account catalog service

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.account.catalogServices.create({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `title` | `string` | No | — |
| `items` | `object[]` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number,
  type?: title | description | item | subtotal | page-break | separator | position-group,
  title?: string,
  description?: string,
  quantity?: number,
  unit?: string,
  unit_price?: number,
  unit_cost?: number,
  net_total?: number,
  optional?: boolean,
  part?: boolean,
  subtotal_type?: part_total | sub_total | optional_part_total | optional_sub_total,
  destroyed?: boolean,
  additional?: boolean,
  position_group_display?: string,
  revenue_category_id?: number | null,
  position_group_items?: {
    title?: string,
    unit?: string,
    quantity?: number,
    unit_price?: number,
    unit_cost?: number
  }[]
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | — |
| `title` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | — |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | — |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  type?: string | null,
  title?: string | null | null,
  description?: string | null | null,
  quantity?: number | null | null,
  unit?: string | null | null,
  unit_price?: number | null | null,
  net_total?: number | null,
  unit_cost?: number | null | null,
  optional?: boolean | null,
  part?: boolean | null,
  subtotal_type?: string | null | null,
  additional?: boolean | null,
  position_group_display?: string | null | null,
  revenue_category?: {
    id?: number | null,
    name?: string | null,
    revenue_account?: number | null | null,
    cost_category?: string | null | null,
    assignment_kind?: string | null
  } | null | null,
  position_group_items?: {
    title?: string | null,
    unit?: string | null,
    quantity?: number | null,
    unit_price?: number | null,
    unit_cost?: number | null
  }[] | null | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
}[] | null
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### catalogServices.delete

`account.catalogServices.delete`

Delete account catalog service

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.account.catalogServices.delete({ id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |



**Output:** `object`

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="Output full type">

```ts

  id?: number | null,
  title?: string | null,
  items?: {
    id?: number | null,
    type?: string | null,
    title?: string | null | null,
    description?: string | null | null,
    quantity?: number | null | null,
    unit?: string | null | null,
    unit_price?: number | null | null,
    net_total?: number | null,
    unit_cost?: number | null | null,
    optional?: boolean | null,
    part?: boolean | null,
    subtotal_type?: string | null | null,
    additional?: boolean | null,
    position_group_display?: string | null | null,
    revenue_category?: {
      id?: number | null,
      name?: string | null,
      revenue_account?: number | null | null,
      cost_category?: string | null | null,
      assignment_kind?: string | null
    } | null | null,
    position_group_items?: {
      title?: string | null,
      unit?: string | null,
      quantity?: number | null,
      unit_price?: number | null,
      unit_cost?: number | null
    }[] | null | null,
    created_at?: string | null,
    updated_at?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
} | {
  success: true
}
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### catalogServices.list

`account.catalogServices.list`

List account catalog services, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.account.catalogServices.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[]` | Yes | One page of results. |
| `next_page` | `number \| null` | Yes | The page to ask for next: set while this page came back full (per_page, default 100), null once it came back short. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number | null,
  title?: string | null,
  items?: {
    id?: number | null,
    type?: string | null,
    title?: string | null | null,
    description?: string | null | null,
    quantity?: number | null | null,
    unit?: string | null | null,
    unit_price?: number | null | null,
    net_total?: number | null,
    unit_cost?: number | null | null,
    optional?: boolean | null,
    part?: boolean | null,
    subtotal_type?: string | null | null,
    additional?: boolean | null,
    position_group_display?: string | null | null,
    revenue_category?: {
      id?: number | null,
      name?: string | null,
      revenue_account?: number | null | null,
      cost_category?: string | null | null,
      assignment_kind?: string | null
    } | null | null,
    position_group_items?: {
      title?: string | null,
      unit?: string | null,
      quantity?: number | null,
      unit_price?: number | null,
      unit_cost?: number | null
    }[] | null | null,
    created_at?: string | null,
    updated_at?: string | null
  }[] | null,
  created_at?: string | null,
  updated_at?: string | null
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>

### create

`activities.create`

Create a time entry on a project task for the authenticated user, in hours or seconds

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.activities.create({
	date: '2026-04-09',
	project_id: 1234567,
	task_id: 7536,
	seconds: 1800,
	description: 'Kickoff call',
	billable: true,
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `impersonate_user_id` | `number` | No | Execute request on behalf of another user if the authenticated user has permission to *Staff*. Some resources, such as Activities and User Presences, can be created for other users only if this header is used. |
| `date` | `string` | No | Activity date. |
| `task_id` | `number` | No | Task/service ID. |
| `company_id` | `number` | No | Optional company/customer ID shortcut. |
| `project_id` | `number` | No | Project ID (mapped internally to assignment). |
| `description` | `string` | No | Free-text activity description. |
| `tag` | `string` | No | Optional activity tag label. |
| `seconds` | `number` | No | Duration in seconds. |
| `hours` | `number` | No | Deprecated duration field, converted to seconds. |
| `billable` | `boolean` | No | Whether the activity is billable. |
| `remote_service` | `"" \| trello \| jira \| asana \| basecamp \| wunderlist \| basecamp2 \| basecamp3 \| toggl \| mite \| github \| youtrack \| null` | No | External source system identifier. |
| `remote_url` | `string` | No | URL to related external item. |
| `remote_id` | `string` | No | External identifier of linked remote item. |
| `stop_timer` | `boolean` | No | If true, stops any running timer before applying the update. This allows updating `seconds` even when a timer is running. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Activity ID. |
| `date` | `string \| null` | No | Activity date. |
| `hours` | `number \| null` | No | Tracked hours as decimal value. |
| `description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Free-text description. |
| `billed` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this activity has been billed. |
| `billable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether this activity is billable. |
| `invoice_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Linked invoice ID if billed. |
| `tag` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Activity tag. |
| `remote_service` | `string \| null \| null` | No | External integration identifier. |
| `remote_id` | `string \| null \| null` | No | External activity ID. |
| `remote_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | External activity URL. |
| `project` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal project reference used in nested resources. |
| `task` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal task reference used in nested resources. |
| `customer` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal company reference used in nested resources. |
| `user` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal user reference used in nested resources. |
| `deal` | `object \| null` | No | — |
| `hourly_rate` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Effective hourly rate if visible to caller. |
| `seconds` | `number \| null` | No | Tracked seconds. |
| `worked_seconds` | `number \| null` | No | Effective worked seconds including rounding logic. |
| `timer_started_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Active timer start timestamp, if timer is running. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp in UTC. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp in UTC. |

| `activity_ids` | `number[]` | Yes | IDs of activities to disregard. |

## Approvals

### approve

`approvals.approve`

Approve approval: approves a pending approval

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.approvals.approve({ id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Approval ID. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | Current approval status. |
| `target_type` | `string \| null` | No | The type of the approval target. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp in UTC. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp in UTC. |
| `decided_at` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Timestamp when the approval decision was made. |
| `note` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Note attached to the approval request. |
| `deny_note` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Note attached when denying the approval. |
| `creator` | `object \| null` | No | Creator of this approval request. |
| `approver` | `object \| null` | No | First assigned user for approval. |
| `approver2` | `object \| null` | No | Second assigned user for approval (optional). |
| `processed_by` | `object \| null` | No | User who approved or denied the approval request. |
| `target` | `object \| null` | No | The invoice or purchase this approval belongs to. |

### list

`approvals.list`

List approvals, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.approvals.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `type` | `invoice \| purchase` | No | Filter by approval target type. |
| `approver_user_id` | `number` | No | Filter by approver user ID. |
| `creator_user_id` | `number` | No | Filter by creator user ID. |
| `approval_status` | `pending \| approved \| denied` | No | Filter by approval status. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[]` | Yes | One page of results. |
| `next_page` | `number \| null` | Yes | The page to ask for next: set while this page came back full (per_page, default 100), null once it came back short. |

### list

`companies.list`

List clients, suppliers and organizations, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.companies.list({ type: 'customer', term: 'beachy' });
```

**Input**

|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `include_archived` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `type` | `customer \| supplier \| organization` | No | — |
| `tags` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of tag names. |
| `identifier` | `string` | No | — |
| `term` | `string` | No | — |
| `custom_properties` | `Record<string, string \| boolean \| string[]>` | No | Filter by custom field values. Unknown custom field names return an empty result set. For MultiSelect fields, pass multiple values using `custom_properties[Field][]=A&custom_properties[Field][]=B`. Boolean fields should be queried with `true` or `false`. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `next_page` | `number \| null` | Yes | The page to ask for next: set while this page came back full (per_page, default 100), null once it came back short. |

```ts
	firstname: 'Jana',
```

### list

`contacts.list`

List contact persons, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.contacts.list({ term: 'jana' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `tags` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of tag names. |
| `term` | `string` | No | Full text search on name, email and company. |
| `phone` | `string` | No | Reverse lookup on work/mobile phone. |
| `custom_properties` | `Record<string, string \| boolean \| string[]>` | No | Filter by custom field values. Unknown custom field names return an empty result set. For MultiSelect fields, pass multiple values using `custom_properties[Field][]=A&custom_properties[Field][]=B`. Boolean fields should be queried with `true` or `false`. |



**Output**

### create

`invoices.create`

Create invoice

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.invoices.create({
	customer_id: 1,
	recipient_address: 'Beachy GmbH
Example Street 1
10115 Berlin',
	date: '2026-04-09',
	due_date: '2026-04-09',
	title: 'Website Relaunch',
	currency: 'EUR',
	items: [{ type: 'title' }],
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `status` | `created \| draft` | No | Initial invoice status. When omitted, the backend applies its default behavior. Set to `draft` to create a draft invoice that is not yet issued and can be edited further. |
| `customer_id` | `number` | Yes | Customer company ID. |
| `offer_id` | `number` | No | Optional source offer ID. |
| `project_id` | `number` | No | Optional project ID. |
| `category` | `default \| partial \| final` | No | Invoice category. When omitted, defaults to `default`. - `default`: Regular invoice. - `partial`: Partial invoice (Abschlagsrechnung). Cannot be combined with a discount. - `final`: Final invoice (Schlussrechnung). |
| `currency` | `string` | Yes | Currency code. |
| `identifier` | `string` | No | Optional custom invoice number. Only applied when the invoice number range is set to manual numbering; then it is required and must be unique within the account. Ignored with automatic numbering and for drafts. |
| `title` | `string` | Yes | Invoice title. |
| `date` | `string` | Yes | Invoice date. |
| `due_date` | `string` | Yes | Due date. |
| `service_period_from` | `string` | No | Service period start. Use together with `service_period_to`. |
| `service_period_to` | `string` | No | Service period end. Use together with `service_period_from`. |
| `salutation` | `string` | No | Salutation text. Supports template variables such as `{salutation}`, `{recipient}`, `{company_name}`, `{date}`, `{due_date}`, and more. |
| `footer` | `string` | No | Footer text. Supports template variables such as `{identifier}`, `{gross_total}`, `{net_total}`, `{user}`, `{me}`, and more. |
| `recipient_address` | `string` | Yes | Recipient address. |
| `change_address` | `invoice \| project \| customer` | No | Replace recipient address from selected source. |
| `discount` | `number` | No | Discount value. |
| `tax` | `number` | No | Deprecated. VAT/tax percentage. Use `vat_code_id` instead. When `vat_code_id` is provided, this field is ignored. |
| `vat_code_id` | `number` | No | VAT code ID for this invoice. Takes precedence over the deprecated `tax` field. Available VAT codes can be fetched via `GET /api/v1/vat_code_sales`. |
| `contact_id` | `number` | No | External contact person ID. |
| `internal_contact_id` | `number` | No | Internal contact user ID. |
| `cash_discount` | `number` | No | Cash discount percentage. |
| `cash_discount_days` | `number` | No | Cash discount validity in days. |
| `print_detail_columns` | `boolean` | No | Include detailed columns in print layouts. |
| `payment_means` | `not_defined \| credit_transfer` | No | Payment means for this invoice. When omitted, derived from the customer's e-invoice configuration (`credit_transfer` for e-invoice customers whose payment means default is bank transfer, otherwise `not_defined`). Must be `credit_transfer` when `payment_account_id` is provided; `payment_account_id` must be omitted otherwise. |
| `payment_account_id` | `number \| null` | No | Payment account receiving the payment. When omitted, derived from the customer's default payment account or the account's default incoming payment account (for e-invoice customers with `credit_transfer`). Only allowed together with `payment_means=credit_transfer`. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Invoice tags. |
| `custom_properties` | `object` | No | Custom field values keyed by custom field name. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number \| null` | No | Letter paper ID to use for this invoice. When omitted, the account's default invoice letter paper is used. If `without_letter_paper` is set to `true`, this field is ignored. Available letter papers can be fetched via `GET /api/v1/letter_papers`. |
| `without_letter_paper` | `boolean` | No | When set to `true`, creates the invoice without letter paper (white). This takes precedence over `letter_paper_id`. |
| `items` | `object[]` | Yes | Invoice line items. |

<AccordionGroup>
<Accordion title="custom_properties full type">

```ts
{
}
```
</Accordion>

<Accordion title="items full type">

```ts
{
  id?: number,
  type: title | description | item | subtotal | page-break | separator,
  title?: string,
  description?: string,
  quantity?: number,
  unit?: string,
  unit_price?: number,
  net_total?: number,
  part?: boolean,
  subtotal_type?: part_total | sub_total,
  additional?: boolean,
  optional?: boolean,
  revenue_category_id?: number,
  activity_ids?: number[],
  expense_ids?: number[]
}[]
```
</Accordion>
</AccordionGroup>



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Internal invoice ID. |
| `customer_id` | `number \| null` | No | Customer company ID. |
| `project_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Linked project ID. |
| `offer_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Linked offer ID. |
| `identifier` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Human-readable invoice number. |
| `date` | `string \| null` | No | Invoice date. |
| `due_date` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Due date. |
| `service_period` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Human-readable service period. |
| `service_period_from` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Service period start date. |
| `service_period_to` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Service period end date. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | Public API status. |
| `reversed` | `boolean \| null \| null` | No | Whether this invoice has been reversed. |
| `reversal_invoice_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Id of the reversal invoice, if any. |
| `reversal` | `boolean \| null \| null` | No | Whether this invoice itself is a reversal invoice. |
| `reversed_invoice_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Id of the invoice that was reversed by this one. |
| `title` | `string \| null` | No | Invoice title/subject. |
| `recipient_address` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Recipient address printed on the invoice. |
| `currency` | `string \| null` | No | Invoice currency. |
| `net_total` | `number \| null` | No | Net total amount. |
| `tax` | `number \| null \| null` | No | VAT/tax percentage. |
| `vat` | `object \| null` | No | Full VAT code block. |
| `gross_total` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Gross total amount. |
| `discount` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Discount value. |
| `cash_discount` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Cash discount percentage. |
| `cash_discount_days` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Number of days cash discount is valid. |
| `creditor_reference` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Creditor reference used for payments. |
| `debit_number` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Bookkeeping debit account number. |
| `credit_number` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Bookkeeping credit account number. |
| `locked` | `boolean \| null \| null` | No | Whether accounting/printing has locked modifications. |
| `custom_properties` | `object \| null` | No | Custom field values keyed by custom field name. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | Invoice tags. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Letter paper ID used for this invoice. Available letter papers can be fetched via `GET /api/v1/letter_papers`. |
| `without_letter_paper` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the invoice is set to be printed without letter paper (white). When `true`, `letter_paper_id` will be `null`. |
| `user` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal user reference used in nested resources. |
| `file_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Download URL to the invoice file. URL is temporary and can change. |
| `debt_collection_procedure` | `object \| null` | No | A debt collection procedure linked to an invoice. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp in UTC. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp in UTC. |
| `created_on` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated date-only version of `created_at`. |
| `updated_on` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated date-only version of `updated_at`. |
| `salutation` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Salutation used in invoice document. |
| `footer` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Footer text used in invoice document. |
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | Invoice line items in display order. |
| `payments` | `object[] \| null` | No | Payments linked to this invoice. |
| `reminders` | `object[] \| null` | No | Payment reminders linked to this invoice. |
| `internal_contact` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal user reference used in nested resources. |
| `activity_hours_modified` | `boolean \| null` | No | Indicates that invoiced activity hours were modified. |

| `due_date` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Due date. |
| `service_period` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Human-readable service period. |
| `service_period_from` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Service period start date. |
| `service_period_to` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Service period end date. |
| `status` | `string \| null` | No | Public API status. |
| `reversed` | `boolean \| null \| null` | No | Whether this invoice has been reversed. |
| `reversal_invoice_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Id of the reversal invoice, if any. |
| `reversal` | `boolean \| null \| null` | No | Whether this invoice itself is a reversal invoice. |
| `reversed_invoice_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Id of the invoice that was reversed by this one. |
| `title` | `string \| null` | No | Invoice title/subject. |
| `recipient_address` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Recipient address printed on the invoice. |
| `currency` | `string \| null` | No | Invoice currency. |
| `net_total` | `number \| null` | No | Net total amount. |
| `tax` | `number \| null \| null` | No | VAT/tax percentage. |
| `vat` | `object \| null` | No | Full VAT code block. |
| `gross_total` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Gross total amount. |
| `discount` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Discount value. |
| `cash_discount` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Cash discount percentage. |
| `cash_discount_days` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Number of days cash discount is valid. |
| `creditor_reference` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Creditor reference used for payments. |
| `debit_number` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Bookkeeping debit account number. |
| `credit_number` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Bookkeeping credit account number. |
| `locked` | `boolean \| null \| null` | No | Whether accounting/printing has locked modifications. |
| `custom_properties` | `object \| null` | No | Custom field values keyed by custom field name. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | Invoice tags. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Letter paper ID used for this invoice. Available letter papers can be fetched via `GET /api/v1/letter_papers`. |
| `without_letter_paper` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the invoice is set to be printed without letter paper (white). When `true`, `letter_paper_id` will be `null`. |
| `user` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal user reference used in nested resources. |
| `file_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Download URL to the invoice file. URL is temporary and can change. |
| `debt_collection_procedure` | `object \| null` | No | A debt collection procedure linked to an invoice. |
| `created_at` | `string \| null` | No | Creation timestamp in UTC. |
| `updated_at` | `string \| null` | No | Last update timestamp in UTC. |
| `created_on` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated date-only version of `created_at`. |
| `updated_on` | `string \| null` | No | Deprecated date-only version of `updated_at`. |
| `salutation` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Salutation used in invoice document. |
| `footer` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Footer text used in invoice document. |
| `items` | `object[] \| null` | No | Invoice line items in display order. |
| `payments` | `object[] \| null` | No | Payments linked to this invoice. |
| `reminders` | `object[] \| null` | No | Payment reminders linked to this invoice. |
| `internal_contact` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal user reference used in nested resources. |
| `activity_hours_modified` | `boolean \| null` | No | Indicates that invoiced activity hours were modified. |

### getPdf

`invoices.getPdf`

Download invoice PDF (a PDF, saved to Files)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.invoices.getPdf({ id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `blank` | `boolean` | No | Return PDF without letter paper. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number` | No | Letter paper ID. Defaults to account default when omitted. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### list

`invoices.list`

List invoices with status, date, client and project filters, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.invoices.list({ status: 'created', date_from: '2026-01-01' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `sort_by` | `string` | No | Field and optional direction, e.g. `title desc`. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `status` | `string` | No | Comma-separated API statuses: `draft`, `created`, `sent`, `partially_paid`, `paid`, `overdue`, `ignored`, `disregarded`. `disregarded` invoices are only returned with `include_disregarded=true`. |
| `include_disregarded` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `not_booked` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `date_from` | `string` | No | Invoice date range start. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-01`). |
| `date_to` | `string` | No | Invoice date range end. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-31`). |
| `service_period_from` | `string` | No | Service period start. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-01`). |
| `service_period_to` | `string` | No | Service period end. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-31`). |
| `tags` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of tag names. |
| `identifier` | `string` | No | — |
| `term` | `string` | No | — |
| `company_id` | `number \| string` | No | Single ID or comma-separated list. |
| `project_id` | `number \| string` | No | Single ID or comma-separated list. |
| `custom_properties` | `Record<string, string \| boolean \| string[]>` | No | Filter by custom field values. Unknown custom field names return an empty result set. For MultiSelect fields, pass multiple values using `custom_properties[Field][]=A&custom_properties[Field][]=B`. Boolean fields should be queried with `true` or `false`. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[]` | Yes | One page of results. |
| `next_page` | `number \| null` | Yes | The page to ask for next: set while this page came back full (per_page, default 100), null once it came back short. |

### payments.create

`invoices.payments.create`

Create invoice payment

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.invoices.payments.create({ date: '2026-04-09', paid_total: 1, invoice_id: 777001 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `date` | `string` | Yes | — |
| `paid_total` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `currency` | `string` | No | — |
| `invoice_id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `payment_account_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `partially_paid` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `description` | `string` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Payment ID. |
| `date` | `string \| null` | No | Payment date. |
| `invoice` | `object \| null` | No | Linked invoice (if available). |

### sendEmail

`invoices.sendEmail`

Email an invoice to the recipients given (sends real email from MOCO)

**Risk:** `destructive` · **Irreversible**

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.invoices.sendEmail({
	id: 777001,
	emails_to: 'billing@example.com',
	subject: 'Your invoice',
	text: 'Please find the invoice attached.',
});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `emails_to` | `string` | No | To recipients as semicolon-separated list. |
| `emails_cc` | `string` | No | CC recipients as semicolon-separated list. |
| `emails_bcc` | `string` | No | BCC recipients as semicolon-separated list. |
| `subject` | `string` | Yes | Email subject. |
| `text` | `string` | Yes | Email body text. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number` | No | Optional letter paper ID used for generated PDF. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `emails_to` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Final `to` recipients (semicolon-separated). |
| `emails_cc` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Final `cc` recipients (semicolon-separated). |
| `emails_bcc` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Final `bcc` recipients (semicolon-separated). |
| `subject` | `string \| null` | No | Email subject. |
| `text` | `string \| null` | No | Email body text. |

### updateStatus

`invoices.updateStatus`

Move an invoice to created, sent, overdue or ignored; locked invoices refuse the change

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.invoices.updateStatus({ id: 777001, status: 'sent' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `status` | `created \| sent \| overdue \| ignored` | Yes | Target API status. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | MOCO accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### create

`offerConfirmations.create`

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.offerConfirmations.create({ offer_id: 456001 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `impersonate_user_id` | `number` | No | Execute request on behalf of another user if the authenticated user has permission to *Staff*. Some resources, such as Activities and User Presences, can be created for other users only if this header is used. |
| `offer_id` | `number` | Yes | ID of the offer this confirmation belongs to. |
| `title` | `string` | No | Confirmation title. Defaults to "Auftragsbestätigung – &#123;offer title&#125;" if omitted. |
| `date` | `string` | No | Confirmation date. Defaults to current date if omitted. |
| `salutation_raw` | `string \| null` | No | Salutation text (raw HTML). Defaults to account default if omitted. |
| `footer_raw` | `string \| null` | No | Footer text (raw HTML). Defaults to account default if omitted. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Internal offer confirmation ID. |
| `date` | `string \| null` | No | Confirmation date. |
| `title` | `string \| null` | No | Confirmation title. |
| `salutation_raw` | `string \| null` | No | Salutation text (raw HTML). |
| `footer_raw` | `string \| null` | No | Footer text (raw HTML). |

| `user` | `object \| null` | No | Minimal user reference used in nested resources. |
| `offer` | `object \| null` | No | Parent offer. |

### getPdf

`offerConfirmations.getPdf`

Download offer confirmation PDF (a PDF, saved to Files)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.offerConfirmations.getPdf({ id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number` | No | Letter paper ID. Defaults to account default when omitted. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### getPdf

`offers.getPdf`

Download offer PDF (a PDF, saved to Files)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.offers.getPdf({ id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `letter_paper_id` | `number` | No | Letter paper ID. Defaults to account default when omitted. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |



---

### list

`offers.list`

List offers, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.offers.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `sort_by` | `string` | No | Field and optional direction, e.g. `title desc`. |
| `status` | `created \| sent \| accepted \| partially_billed \| billed \| archived` | No | — |
| `from` | `string` | No | Offer date range start. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-01`). |
| `to` | `string` | No | Offer date range end. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-31`). |
| `identifier` | `string` | No | — |
| `deal_id` | `number \| string` | No | Single ID or comma-separated list. |
| `project_id` | `number \| string` | No | Single ID or comma-separated list. |
| `company_id` | `number \| string` | No | Single ID or comma-separated list. |
| `tags` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of tag names. |
| `custom_properties` | `Record<string, string \| boolean \| string[]>` | No | Filter by custom field values. Unknown custom field names return an empty result set. ```

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|

| `status` | `created \| sent \| accepted \| partially_billed \| billed \| archived` | Yes | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | MOCO accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### create

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.plannedPurchases.create({
	date: '2026-04-09',
	title: 'Website Relaunch',
	net_total: 1,
	currency: 'EUR',
	user_id: 933590696,
});
```

| `finish_date` | `string` | No | — |
| `frequency` | `once \| weekly \| biweekly \| monthly \| quarterly \| biannual \| annual` | No | — |
| `title` | `string` | Yes | — |

### create

`projects.create`

Create a project for a client

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.projects.create({ name: 'Website Relaunch', company_id: 889, currency: 'EUR' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `identifier` | `string` | No | Project number/identifier. Required when number ranges are manual. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Project name. |
| `fixed_price` | `boolean` | No | Whether the project is fixed price. |
| `company_id` | `number` | No | Customer company ID. |
| `customer_id` | `number` | No | Deprecated alias of `company_id`. |
| `deal_id` | `number` | No | Linked deal ID. |
| `project_group_id` | `number` | No | Linked project group ID. |
| `leader_id` | `number` | No | Main responsible user ID. |
| `co_leader_id` | `number` | No | Secondary responsible user ID. |
| `contact_id` | `number` | No | Main external contact ID. |
| `secondary_contact_id` | `number` | No | Secondary external contact ID. |

| `retainer_billing_date` | `number` | No | Retainer billing day in month (1-31). |
| `retainer_billing_title` | `string` | No | Default title for generated retainer items. |
| `retainer_billing_description` | `string` | No | Default description for generated retainer items. |
| `billing_variant` | `project \| task \| user \| billing_variant_rate_per_project \| billing_variant_rate_per_task \| billing_variant_rate_per_user` | No | Pricing mode for hourly rates. Preferred values are `project`, `task`, `user`. `billing_variant_rate_per_project`, `billing_variant_rate_per_task`, `billing_variant_rate_per_user` are also accepted. |

### expenses.create

`projects.expenses.create`

Create project expense

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.projects.expenses.create({ project_id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `project_id` | `number` | Yes | Project ID. |
| `user_id` | `number` | No | Responsible user ID (must be active on create). |
| `date` | `string` | No | Expense date. |
| `title` | `string` | No | Expense title. |
| `description` | `string` | No | Expense description text. |
| `quantity` | `number` | No | Quantity. |
| `unit` | `string` | No | Unit label. |
| `unit_price` | `number` | No | Unit selling price. |
| `price` | `number` | No | Deprecated total selling price alias. |
| `unit_cost` | `number` | No | Unit internal cost. |
| `billable` | `boolean` | No | Whether this expense can be billed. |
| `budget_relevant` | `boolean` | No | Whether this expense affects project budget. |
| `purchase_assignment_locked` | `boolean` | No | Locks assignment to purchase items. |
| `service_period_from` | `string` | No | Start of service period. |
| `service_period_to` | `string` | No | End of service period. |
| `planned_purchase_date` | `string` | No | Planned purchase date (when planned purchases are enabled). |
| `cost_total_planned` | `number` | No | Planned internal cost total. |
| `revenue_category_id` | `number` | No | Revenue category ID for billing assignment. Rejected with 422 when the revenue category only applies to tasks, unless the stored value is unchanged. |
| `custom_properties` | `object` | No | Custom field values keyed by custom field name. |
| `file` | `object` | No | Optional attachment payload. |

### expenses.disregard

`projects.expenses.disregard`

```ts

	project_id: 1234567,
	reason: 'Created by mistake',
	expense_ids: [1],
});
```

| `project_id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `reason` | `string` | Yes | Reason shown in history/audit context. |
| `expense_ids` | `number[]` | Yes | IDs of expenses to disregard. |

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | MOCO accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### get

`projects.get`

Read one project, optionally embedding the client

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.projects.get({ id: 1234567, include_company: true });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `include_company` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number \| null` | No | Internal project ID. |
| `identifier` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Human-readable project number/identifier. |
| `name` | `string \| null` | No | Project name. |
| `active` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the project is active. |
| `billable` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether project work is billable. |
| `fixed_price` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the project is fixed price. |
| `retainer` | `boolean \| null` | No | Whether the project is a retainer project. |
| `start_date` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Project start date in ISO-8601 date format. |
| `finish_date` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Project finish date in ISO-8601 date format. |
| `currency` | `string \| null` | No | Billing currency of the project. |
| `billing_variant` | `string \| null \| null` | No | How rates are applied: - `project`: one project rate - `task`: per task rate - `user`: per assigned user rate |
| `billing_address` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Billing address used for documents. |
| `billing_email_to` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Default recipient email(s) for billing. |
| `billing_email_cc` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Default CC recipient email(s) for billing. |
| `billing_notes` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Internal or document billing notes. |
| `setting_include_time_report` | `boolean \| null` | No | If true, include time report on generated billing documents. |
| `budget` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Budget amount in project currency. |
| `budget_monthly` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Monthly budget for retainers. |
| `budget_expenses` | `number \| null \| null` | No | Expense budget amount. |
| `hourly_rate` | `number \| null` | No | Effective hourly rate according to billing variant. |

| `retainer_billing_title` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Default title used for retainer schedule items. |
| `retainer_billing_description` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Default description used for retainer schedule items. |
| `color` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Project color in hex format. |
| `customer_report_url` | `string \| null \| null` | No | Public customer report URL when sharing is enabled. |
| `tags` | `string[] \| null` | No | Tag names assigned to this project. |
| `labels` | `string[] \| null` | No | Deprecated alias for `tags`. |

### update

`projects.update`

Update a project

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.projects.update({ id: 1234567, name: 'Website Relaunch 2026' });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `identifier` | `string` | No | Project number/identifier. Required when number ranges are manual. |
| `name` | `string` | No | Project name. |
| `fixed_price` | `boolean` | No | Whether the project is fixed price. |
| `company_id` | `number` | No | Customer company ID. |
| `customer_id` | `number` | No | Deprecated alias of `company_id`. |
| `deal_id` | `number` | No | Linked deal ID. |
| `project_group_id` | `number` | No | Linked project group ID. |
| `leader_id` | `number` | No | Main responsible user ID. |
| `co_leader_id` | `number` | No | Secondary responsible user ID. |
| `contact_id` | `number` | No | Main external contact ID. |
| `secondary_contact_id` | `number` | No | Secondary external contact ID. |
| `billing_contact_id` | `number` | No | Billing contact ID. |
| `info` | `string` | No | Internal project notes. |
| `start_date` | `string` | No | Project start date. |
| `finish_date` | `string` | No | Project finish date. |
| `retainer` | `boolean` | No | Whether project is billed as retainer. |
| `retainer_billing_date` | `number` | No | Retainer billing day in month (1-31). |
| `retainer_billing_title` | `string` | No | Default title for generated retainer items. |
| `retainer_billing_description` | `string` | No | Default description for generated retainer items. |
| `billing_variant` | `project \| task \| user \| billing_variant_rate_per_project \| billing_variant_rate_per_task \| billing_variant_rate_per_user` | No | Pricing mode for hourly rates. Preferred values are `project`, `task`, `user`. `billing_variant_rate_per_project`, `billing_variant_rate_per_task`, `billing_variant_rate_per_user` are also accepted. |
| `hourly_rate` | `number` | No | Effective hourly rate for project pricing. |
| `budget` | `number` | No | Project budget in project currency. |
| `budget_monthly` | `number` | No | Monthly budget for retainers. |
| `budget_expenses` | `number` | No | Expense budget in project currency. |
| `currency` | `string` | No | Project currency. Mandatory on create and immutable afterwards. |
| `billing_address` | `string` | No | Billing address for generated documents. |
| `billing_notes` | `string` | No | Notes printed or used during billing. |
| `setting_include_time_report` | `boolean` | No | Include time report in billing output. |
| `billing_email_to` | `string` | No | Default recipient email(s), semicolon-separated. |
| `billing_email_cc` | `string` | No | Default CC email(s), semicolon-separated. |
| `skip_favorite` | `boolean` | No | Do not add this project to favorites for the creator. |
| `tags` | `string[]` | No | Project tags. |
| `labels` | `string[]` | No | Deprecated alias for `tags`. |
| `custom_properties` | `object` | No | Custom field values keyed by custom field name. |

## Purchases

### assignToProject

`purchases.assignToProject`

Assign purchase item to project: assigns one purchase item to a project expense

**Risk:** `write`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.purchases.assignToProject({ id: 1234567, project_id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |
| `item_id` | `number` | No | Single purchase item ID. |
| `project_id` | `number` | Yes | — |
| `expense_id` | `number` | No | Existing expense ID to attach to. |
| `notify_project_leader` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `billable` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `budget_relevant` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `surcharge` | `number` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `success` | `true` | Yes | MOCO accepted the call; it answered with no body. |

### drafts.getPdf

`purchases.drafts.getPdf`

Download purchase draft PDF (a PDF, saved to Files)

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.purchases.drafts.getPdf({ id: 1234567 });
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `id` | `number` | Yes | Resource ID. |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `contentBase64` | `string` | No | The file, base64-encoded. Missing when the file came back as text or contentOmitted is set. |
| `text` | `string` | No | The file as text, in place of contentBase64, when it is a text file (CSV, JSON, Markdown, XML and the like) in UTF-8. |
| `contentType` | `string` | Yes | Media type of the file, for example application/pdf. |
| `byteLength` | `number` | Yes | Size of the file in bytes. |
| `filename` | `string` | No | The file name, when known. |
| `contentOmitted` | `not_requested \| over_limit` | No | Set when the content was left out: the caller did not ask for it, or the file was over the limit. |
| `fileId` | `string` | No | Where the host saved the file. Over the limit it comes in place of the content; a host that keeps every file sets it beside the content too. On Corsair it is a Files id (fil_…): pass it to another app wherever that app takes a file URL, or read a text file or PDF with files.read_text. |

### list

`purchases.list`

List purchases, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.purchases.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `id` | `string` | No | Legacy alias for filtering by purchase ID (single ID or comma-separated list). |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `custom_properties` | `Record<string, string \| boolean \| string[]>` | No | Filter by custom field values. Unknown custom field names return an empty result set. For MultiSelect fields, pass multiple values using `custom_properties[Field][]=A&custom_properties[Field][]=B`. Boolean fields should be queried with `true` or `false`. |
| `category_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `term` | `string` | No | — |
| `company_id` | `number` | No | Supplier company ID. Use `0` for purchases without supplier. |
| `status` | `pending \| archived` | No | — |
| `approval_status` | `none \| approved \| denied \| pending` | No | — |
| `approver_user_id` | `number` | No | — |
| `not_booked` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `tags` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of tag names. |
| `tag_ids` | `string` | No | Comma-separated list of tag IDs. |
| `date` | `string` | No | Use `date_from`/`date_to` instead. Date range, e.g. `2020-02-01:2020-02-22`. |
| `date_from` | `string` | No | Purchase date range start. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-01`). See also date_to. |
| `date_to` | `string` | No | Purchase date range end. Format: YYYY-MM-DD (e.g. `2026-01-31`). See also date_from. |
| `unpaid` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `partially_paid` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `payment_method` | `bank_transfer \| direct_debit \| credit_card \| paypal \| cash \| bank_transfer_swiss_qr_esr` | No | — |
| `receipt_identifier` | `string` | No | — |
| `identifier` | `string` | No | — |
| `user_id` | `number` | No | User ID (responsible for purchase) |

### list

`vatCodeSales.list`

List VAT code sales, one page at a time

**Risk:** `read`

```ts
await corsair.moco.api.vatCodeSales.list({});
```

**Input**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `page` | `number` | No | Page number, starting at 1. |
| `per_page` | `number` | No | Page size. |
| `ids` | `number \| string` | No | Comma-separated IDs. |
| `updated_after` | `string` | No | ISO 8601 UTC timestamp (e.g. `2026-01-31T14:30:00Z`). Returns entities created or updated after this timestamp and is intended for incremental synchronization. Combine with delete webhooks for full synchronization. |
| `active` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `intra_eu` | `boolean` | No | — |
| `reverse_charge` | `boolean` | No | — |



**Output**

| Name | Type | Required | Description |
|------|------|----------|-------------|
| `items` | `object[]` | Yes | One page of results. |
| `next_page` | `number \| null` | Yes | The page to ask for next: set while this page came back full (per_page, default 100), null once it came back short. |

```ts
}[]
```
